| `EF13` | Environment loading & [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) (`PF5`) | Partial (`PF5`) | (`EF13a`) [`infra/load_env.sh`](../infra/load_env.sh) (`line 20`) uses `${BASH_SOURCE[0]}`, which expands to empty when sourced from `zsh` (`macos`), looking for `.env` in the parent directory unless replaced with `${BASH_SOURCE[0]:-${(%):-%x}}`. (`EF13b`) [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) (`line 92`) leaves `# enable_nist_compliance = true` commented out, so a direct `terraform apply -var-file=staging.tfvars` sets `local.any_compliance_active = false` (`main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `lines 384–388`), bypassing `resource "terraform_data" "control_plane_access_guard"` (`main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `lines 1388–1406`) and latching `0.0.0.0/0` via `ignore_changes` in [`gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) (`lines 312–319`) | `POAM-2026-164` (`EF13a` in `Step A3`); `POAM-2026-174` (`EF13b` commented-out `enable_nist_compliance` + `gcp_gke_cluster` `ignore_changes` latch; `database_encryption` split into `POAM-2026-182`) |

   - **Audit of `lifecycle { ignore_changes }` blocks across `infra/` (`2026-11-01`, updated `2026-11-05`):** After `CP-6` (`POAM-2026-173`) deleted the Memorystore `ignore_changes` block from `c58b0913:infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/main.tf` (`lines 78–81`, `resource "google_memorystore_instance" "instance"`), a repository-wide grep of `ignore_changes` across `infra/` leaves **six** blocks across **two** files: (1) [`infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) (`lines 312–319`, `resource "google_container_cluster" "primary"`, ignoring `[node_version, database_encryption, master_authorized_networks_config, min_master_version, monitoring_config, resource_labels]`) — `master_authorized_networks_config` and `monitoring_config` are tracked in **`POAM-2026-174`**, while `database_encryption` is a required `hashicorp/google` `v6.50.0` provider-enum workaround tracked in **`POAM-2026-182`**; (2–5) [`infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) on the four node pools (`resource "google_container_node_pool" "primary_nodes"` at `lines 402–404`, `resource "google_container_node_pool" "general_spot_nodes"` at `lines 476–478`, and `resource "google_container_node_pool" "clickhouse_nodes"` at `lines 682–684`, each ignoring `[initial_node_count]`; and `resource "google_container_node_pool" "gpu_nodes"` at `lines 596–604`, ignoring `[initial_node_count, node_locations]`); and (6) [`infra/modules/vllm_inference/main.tf`](../infra/modules/vllm_inference/main.tf) (`lines 324–326`, `resource "kubernetes_deployment" "vllm"`, ignoring `[spec[0].replicas]`). The four node-pool `initial_node_count`/`node_locations` blocks and the vLLM `spec[0].replicas` block prevent `terraform apply` from resetting live autoscaler replica counts and do not mask any security or compliance control, so they do not require a POA&M entry.

4. **Why the inspection-derived `PF` list missed 11 of 13 findings:** The `PF` list was produced by reading code and enumerating what a fresh project *lacks* (APIs, state bucket, SAs, KMS keys). Most of the real failures (`EF1`, `EF4`, `EF5`, `EF6`, `EF8`, `EF9`, `EF10a`, `EF10b`) were code that is invalid against the live GCP/GKE APIs on *any* project (`21 > 20` batch limit, `count` depending on a computed attribute, `kubernetes_manifest` planning without a cluster client config, removed `"WORKLOADS"` monitoring enum, retired GKE `1.28`, nonexistent `compliance-bridge-sa`, missing `gcp-sa-memorystore` CMEK binding). Code inspection cannot catch those because the inspector reads the code as meaning what it says (`POAM-2026-120` one level up). The one prediction that fully paid off (`PF9` / `EF12`) came from operational experience with `POAM-2026-159`'s retention lock rather than from reading code. **Conclusion:** retire the `PF` list and drive `Step A3` off `EF1`–`EF13`.

**Remediation Plan:**
1. ✅ **Step A2 (Manual Clean-Project Walk — completed `2026-11-01`–`2026-11-02`):** Walked throwaway project `<WALK_PROJECT_ID>` from zero through Stage 1 `terraform apply`, post-Stage-1 state import, Stage 2 `terraform plan` (`159 to add, 2 to change, 0 to destroy`), and post-retention project deletion (`DELETE_REQUESTED` on `2026-11-02T01:36:54Z`), recording `EF1`–`EF13` above and opening `POAM-2026-173` and `POAM-2026-174` for the two live staging posture defects.
2. **Step A3 (Remediate `EF1`–`EF13` and Re-Verify on a Clean Project — outstanding):** Fix the clean-project defects in [`scripts/bootstrap_gcp_project.sh`](../scripts/bootstrap_gcp_project.sh), [`scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh), [`scripts/gen_tfvars.py`](../scripts/gen_tfvars.py), [`infra/load_env.sh`](../infra/load_env.sh), [`.env.example`](../.env.example), [`infra/QUICK_START.md`](../infra/QUICK_START.md), [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/), and [`infra/modules/`](../infra/modules/) driven by `EF1`–`EF13` (retiring the `PF` taxonomy), and re-verify on a clean project.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. ✅ `Step A2` empirical walk findings (`EF1`–`EF13`), `PF` scorecard, and post-retention project deletion (`DELETE_REQUESTED` on `2026-11-02T01:36:54Z` after deleting the `storage.googleapis.com` project lien) recorded here (`2026-11-02`).
2. Every clean-project script, Terraform, and documentation divergence in `EF1`–`EF13` is remediated and merged on `main` (`Step A3`), and [`scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh) reflects the empirically verified two-stage apply, post-Stage-1 state import, and post-retention lien/project deletion sequence.
3. A clean-project run after `Step A3` merges completes without manual workarounds, and its merge commit SHAs and verification date are recorded here.

### POAM-2026-173: Orphaned `module.vpc_network` (`cage-vpc-staging`) and Memorystore `ignore_changes` Migration Trap

**Control:** NIST SC-7 (Boundary Protection), CM-6 (Configuration Settings)
**Risk Level:** Moderate (re-rated `2026-11-05` from High by the `082` test: internal VPC boundary defect with no internet-facing or cross-project credential exposure)
**Status:** Open — verified live on `<PROJECT_ID>` (`2026-11-01`, `EF7a`); Finding C (`F-10`) deleted in `#681` (`1f8641f6`, `DE-1`), scope narrowed to 5 consumers (`AR-1` / `POAM-2026-189` retires `module.gcp_geap_advisor` from `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`), and IaC remediated on `fix/vpc-network-binding` (`CP-6`), pending merge, Step A3 clean-project walk, and live staging rebuild (`W`)
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-02
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
`c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/perimeter.tf` (`lines 23–38`) provisioned `module.vpc_network` (`source = "../../modules/vpc_network"`, `count = var.create_vpc_network ? 1 : 0`, default `true`), which creates a custom-mode VPC (`cage-vpc-${var.environment}`) with a primary subnet (`cage-subnet-${var.environment}`, [`infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf`](../infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf) `lines 26, 52–68`) and `gke-pods`/`gke-services` secondary IP ranges, Cloud Router and Cloud NAT (`lines 72–91`), Private Services Access peering (`google_service_networking_connection.private_service_access`, `line 165`), and three SC-7 firewall rules (`google_compute_firewall.default_deny_ingress` → `${local.network_name}-default-deny-all-ingress`, `google_compute_firewall.allow_internal` → `${local.network_name}-allow-internal`, and `google_compute_firewall.allow_https_egress` → `${local.network_name}-allow-https-egress`, `lines 95–150`).

Prior to `CP-6` (`fix/vpc-network-binding`), `c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` wired `module.vpc_network` into **nothing**:
1. **All five remaining workload consumers used `var.network` and `var.subnetwork` instead of `module.vpc_network` (`EF7a`; `AR-1` / `POAM-2026-189` retires the sixth, `module.gcp_geap_advisor` in `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`):**
   - `module.gke` (`c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` `lines 147–148`): `network = var.network`, `subnetwork = var.subnetwork`.
   - `module.service_mesh` (`c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` `lines 179–195`): omitted `network`, so `resource "google_compute_firewall" "mesh_webhooks"` (`cage-mesh-webhooks-${var.environment}` in [`infra/modules/service_mesh/main.tf`](../infra/modules/service_mesh/main.tf) `lines 204–216`) defaulted `var.network` to `"default"`.
   - `module.cloudsql_postgres` (`c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` `line 399`): `authorized_network = var.network` (expanded to `private_network = local.network_link` in [`infra/modules/cloudsql_postgres/main.tf`](../infra/modules/cloudsql_postgres/main.tf) `lines 27, 57`).
   - `module.memorystore_governance` (`c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` `line 433`) and `module.memorystore_app` (`line 469`): `network_id = var.network`.
   - `module.gcp_geap_advisor` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` `lines 1069–1070`, retired separately under `POAM-2026-189`): `vpc_network = var.network`, `vpc_subnetwork = var.subnetwork`.
   - `c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf` (`lines 1064–1074`) defaulted `network = "default"` and `subnetwork = "default"`, and none of the committed `.tfvars` profiles overrode `network` or `subnetwork`.
2. **Live verification on `<PROJECT_ID>` (`2026-11-01`, read-only `gcloud` inspection):**
   - Both VPCs (`default` in `AUTO` subnet mode and `cage-vpc-staging` in `CUSTOM` subnet mode) exist in `<PROJECT_ID>`.
   - `cage-vpc-staging` holds its subnet (`cage-subnet-staging`, `10.0.0.0/20`), Cloud NAT, PSA peering range (`cage-vpc-staging-psa`), and its 3 SC-7 firewall rules (`cage-vpc-staging-default-deny-all-ingress`, `cage-vpc-staging-allow-internal`, `cage-vpc-staging-allow-https-egress`), and **0 attached workloads**.
   - Every live workload — GKE cluster `cage-staging` (`networkConfig.network = ".../networks/default"`), Cloud SQL `cage-postgres-staging` (`ipConfiguration.privateNetwork = ".../networks/default"`), Memorystore Valkey `cage-valkey-gov-staging-v2` and `cage-valkey-app-staging-v3` (`pscAutoConnections[0].network = ".../networks/default"`), and `cage-mesh-webhooks-staging` — runs on the `default` VPC, whose firewall rules include `default-allow-internal` (`all` protocols from `10.128.0.0/9`) and `allow-ssh-ingress-from-iap` (`tcp:22`) and **no** default-deny ingress rule.
   - `POAM-2026-169` instance 1 corrected only the false [`TECH_STACK.md`](architecture/TECH_STACK.md) claim, not the infrastructure defect. [`test_gke_sole_target_6f.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_gke_sole_target_6f.py) (`lines 1112–1119`, `test_perimeter_tf_wires_vpc_binauthz_vpcsc_cloud_armor_and_cloud_dns`) originally asserted only `'module "vpc_network"' in perimeter_tf` and never checked whether any module referenced `module.vpc_network[0].network_name` or `module.vpc_network[0].subnetwork_name` — another instance of `POAM-2026-120`.
3. **The Memorystore `ignore_changes` migration trap (`c58b0913:infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/main.tf` `lines 78–81`, removed in `CP-6`):**
   - `resource "google_memorystore_instance" "instance"` (`line 31`) defined:
     ```hcl
     lifecycle {
       ignore_changes = [
         desired_psc_auto_connections,
         desired_auto_created_endpoints,
       ]
     }
     ```
   - `local.network_link` (`line 28`) fed only `desired_psc_auto_connections` (`lines 72–75`). Because `desired_psc_auto_connections` was in `ignore_changes`, changing `var.network` (or wiring `module.vpc_network[0].network_name` into `module.memorystore_governance` and `module.memorystore_app`) planned clean (`0 to add, 0 to change, 0 to destroy`) and left both Memorystore Valkey instances attached to the `default` VPC.
4. **Finding C (`F-10`, added `2026-11-05`, deleted in `#681` `1f8641f6`): `staging.tfvars` copied the `default` VPC's subnet and PSA CIDRs (`10.128.0.0/20`, `10.6.80.0/20`):**
   - `c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars` (`lines 225–227`) set `memorystore_governance_psc_cidr = "10.128.0.0/20"`, `memorystore_app_psc_cidr = "10.128.0.0/20"`, and `cloud_sql_cidr = "10.6.80.0/20"`, copied from the `default` VPC's `us-central1` subnet (`10.128.0.0/20`) and PSA allocation (`10.6.80.0/20`) in commit `36b6bd60` (`#323`, `2026-09-29`). `PR-1` (`#681`, `1f8641f6`, `DE-1` under `POAM-2026-190`) deleted those three static `*_cidr` variables from [`variables.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf) and [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) and derived `/32` endpoint CIDRs directly from module outputs.

**Remediation Plan:**
1. ✅ **IaC, Bootstrap & Plan-Time Contract Tests (`#681` `1f8641f6` + `fix/vpc-network-binding`, `CP-6`, pending merge):**
   - [`infra/modules/vpc_network/`](../infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf) provisions `google_network_connectivity_service_connection_policy.memorystore` (`service_class = "gcp-memorystore"`, gated independently on `var.enable_memorystore_psc`, decoupled from `var.enable_psa`), exposes readiness-gated `private_service_access_network_name` (`EF7b`) and `memorystore_psc_network_name` (`EF7c`) outputs plus subnet-backed `pod_secondary_range_name` / `service_secondary_range_name` outputs, and retains Cloud NAT `ALL` logging and `INCLUDE_ALL_METADATA` logging on all three `SC-7` firewall rules.
   - [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/perimeter.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/perimeter.tf) and `main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) bind all five custom-VPC consumers (`module.gke`, `module.service_mesh` `google_compute_firewall.mesh_webhooks`, `module.cloudsql_postgres` via `local.cloudsql_network_name`, and `module.memorystore_governance` / `module.memorystore_app` via `local.memorystore_network_name`) to `module.vpc_network[0]` in create mode and to `var.network` / `var.subnetwork` in BYO mode (with no-discard secondary-range preconditions in [`gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf)), remove the three redundant `depends_on = [module.gke]` blocks on Cloud SQL and both Memorystores, and leave `module.gcp_geap_advisor` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`) unchanged pending its retirement in `POAM-2026-189`.
   - [`infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/main.tf`](../infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/main.tf) replaces deprecated `desired_psc_auto_connections` with `desired_auto_created_endpoints` and deletes `lifecycle.ignore_changes` entirely (`c58b0913:infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/main.tf` `lines 78–81`), and [`scripts/bootstrap_gcp_project.sh`](../scripts/bootstrap_gcp_project.sh) grants `roles/networkconnectivity.consumerNetworkAdmin` to `--deployer-sa`.
   - Guarded by [`vpc_binding.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/vpc_binding.tftest.hcl) (`9` plan-time contract runs covering create mode, BYO named/CIDR modes, discarded-CIDR refusals, Memorystore `desired_auto_created_endpoints`, PSA/SCP flag independence, and `DE-1` `/32` egress derivation across the move), [`test_gke_sole_target_6f.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_gke_sole_target_6f.py), [`test_memorystore_topology.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_memorystore_topology.py), and [`test_gcp_project_bootstrap.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_gcp_project_bootstrap.py).
2. **Live Verification (`Step A3` Clean-Project Walk & Staging Rebuild `W` — outstanding):** Execute the `Step A3` clean-project walk (`POAM-2026-164`) and rebuild live staging onto `cage-vpc-staging` (`W`, `§W`), verifying custom-VPC attachment, active firewall + Cloud NAT logging (`SC-7`, `R-5`, `F-87`), and end-to-end gateway → governance Valkey connectivity.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. Merge commit SHAs (`#681` `1f8641f6` + `PR-2`) wiring `module.vpc_network` into all five custom-VPC consumers (`module.gcp_geap_advisor` in `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` tracked under `POAM-2026-189`), deleting the static `*_cidr` copies from [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars), replacing `desired_psc_auto_connections` + deleting `ignore_changes` in [`memorystore_valkey/main.tf`](../infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/main.tf), granting `roles/networkconnectivity.consumerNetworkAdmin` in [`bootstrap_gcp_project.sh`](../scripts/bootstrap_gcp_project.sh), and adding [`vpc_binding.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/vpc_binding.tftest.hcl) and strengthened [`test_gke_sole_target_6f.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_gke_sole_target_6f.py) checks are recorded here.
2. `gcloud` and runtime verification output from the `Step A3` walk / live staging rebuild (`W`, `§W`), recorded here with the date, shows: (a) the GKE cluster, `cage-mesh-webhooks-staging` firewall rule, Cloud SQL instance, both Memorystore Valkey instances, and `google_network_connectivity_service_connection_policy.memorystore` on `cage-vpc-staging` rather than `default` (with zero manual out-of-band objects required); (b) active `cage-vpc-staging` firewall rules (`INCLUDE_ALL_METADATA`) and Cloud NAT (`ALL`) emitting `SC-7` boundary logs in Cloud Logging (`R-5`, `F-87`); and (c) a live gateway → governance Valkey round trip succeeding over the custom-VPC PSC endpoint.

### POAM-2026-174: Commented-Out `enable_nist_compliance` in `staging.tfvars` and GKE `ignore_changes` Control-Plane Latch

**Control:** NIST CM-6 (Configuration Settings), CP-9 (System Backup), AU-9 (Protection of Audit Information), AC-3 (Access Enforcement), AU-12 (Audit Generation)
**Risk Level:** High
**Status:** Open. Item 1 remediated `2026-11-03`, pending merge (Remediation Plan step 1). Item 2 open: all six `ignore_changes` entries in [`gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) (`lines 312–319`) were empirically classified on `2026-11-04` (`database_encryption` split into `POAM-2026-182`; `min_master_version` removed from item 2 as ordinary GKE `REGULAR` release-channel practice; `master_authorized_networks_config` and `monitoring_config` retained as the two security-attribute latches in item 2, blocked by `POAM-2026-183` and `EF10a` in `POAM-2026-164` respectively). Originally verified in [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) (`line 92`) and [`gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) (`lines 312–319`) during `Step A2` (`EF13b`, `2026-11-01`)
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-02
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **`staging.tfvars` left `# enable_nist_compliance = true` commented out (`EF13b`; remediated, step 1):**
   - [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) (`line 92`) contained `# enable_nist_compliance = true` (commented out), while [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf) (`lines 62–66`) defaults `enable_nist_compliance = false`.
   - When [`deploy_all.sh`](../deploy_all.sh) (`lines 426–442`) ran for `US_FED`, it injected `-var="enable_nist_compliance=true"` on the command line (and [`scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh) `lines 547, 1097–1103` invoked `deploy_all.sh` for provisioning and passed `-var=enable_nist_compliance=true` in `DESTROY_ARGS`). However, when an operator invoked `terraform plan`, `terraform apply`, or `terraform destroy -var-file=staging.tfvars` directly (the profile's `lines 54–55` document the direct `terraform destroy`), `enable_nist_compliance` evaluated to `false`, and `any_compliance_active` (`main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `lines 384–388`) evaluated to `false`.
   - With `local.any_compliance_active = false`:
     - `resource "terraform_data" "control_plane_access_guard"` (`main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `lines 1388–1406`) and the `POAM-019` telemetry key guards (`resource "terraform_data" "poam_019_empty_key_guard"` and `resource "terraform_data" "poam_019_shared_key_guard"` at `main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `lines 1246–1293`) are bypassed (`!local.any_compliance_active || ...`), and `module.gke` (`enable_nist_compliance = local.any_compliance_active` at `main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `line 93`) sets `master_authorized_networks_config` to `0.0.0.0/0` (`"All (Dev Only)"` at [`infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) `lines 168–176`);
     - `module.cloudsql_postgres` (`main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) `line 410`) sets `enable_point_in_time_recovery = false` (`var.environment == "prod" || local.any_compliance_active`), silently disabling Cloud SQL PITR in place.
   - The code quoted above is the code at the finding. Since `POAM-2026-181` these sites read `local.enforcing_posture`, and `POAM-2026-130` removed the three compliance flags and `any_compliance_active`.
2. **Empirical classification of all six `ignore_changes` entries on `google_container_cluster.primary` ([`infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) `lines 312–319`; verified `2026-11-04`):**
   - `resource "google_container_cluster" "primary"` defines:
     ```hcl
     lifecycle {
       ignore_changes = [
         node_version,
         database_encryption, # POAM-2026-182: provider 6.50.0 enum lacks ALL_OBJECTS_ENCRYPTION_ENABLED
         master_authorized_networks_config,
         min_master_version,
         monitoring_config,
         resource_labels,
       ]
     }
     ```
   - On `2026-11-04`, each of the six entries was tested individually by running `terraform plan -refresh=false -target=module.gke.google_container_cluster.primary` with `ignore_changes = []` against `gs://<PROJECT_ID>-tfstate/cage/gcp-gke/cage-staging/default.tfstate` (`serial: 695`), `gs://<PROJECT_ID>-tfstate/backups/cage-gcp-gke/default.tfstate.pre-migration-backup` (`serial: 689`), and `<PROJECT_ID>` Cloud Audit Logs (`2026-09-06`–`2026-10-29`):

| Attribute ([`gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf)) | Plan diff with `ignore_changes = []` against `cage-staging` (`serial: 695`) | Cloud Audit Log & state evidence (`<PROJECT_ID>`, `2026-09-06`–`2026-10-29`) | Classification & disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| `node_version` (`line 295`) | None (`0` diff; unset in HCL) | Live state holds `"1.35.8-gke.1796000"`. GKE's `REGULAR` release channel (`UpdateClusterInternal`) auto-upgraded the node pools 12 times between `2026-09-15` and `2026-10-28` | **Ordinary GKE `REGULAR` release-channel practice (KEEP in `ignore_changes`).** Not a security latch |
| `database_encryption` (`line 296`) | `~ state = "ALL_OBJECTS_ENCRYPTION_ENABLED" -> "ENCRYPTED"` (`key_name` unchanged) | Four consecutive ~9-minute `UpdateCluster` runs on `2026-09-29` (`02:47:06Z`–`03:39:33Z`) sent `state = "ENCRYPTED"` and received `"ALL_OBJECTS_ENCRYPTION_ENABLED"` back each time; `hashicorp/google` `v6.50.0` rejects `"ALL_OBJECTS_ENCRYPTION_ENABLED"` | **Required provider-enum workaround (`POAM-2026-182`, KEEP in `ignore_changes` until `hashicorp/google` adds the enum).** Guarded by [`test_gke_database_encryption_workaround.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_gke_database_encryption_workaround.py) |

Commit `36b6bd60` (`2026-09-29T17:31:28Z`, `#323`) added `master_authorized_networks_config` to `ignore_changes`, latching `cage-staging` at `0.0.0.0/0` for **29 days** until manual `gcloud container clusters update` calls on `2026-10-28T19:18:17Z` and `2026-10-29T22:19:40Z` overwrote it out-of-band with `136.115.30.143/32`, `10.128.0.70/32`, and `108.31.95.35/32` (`displayName = ""`) | **Security-attribute latch (AC-3 / SC-7; REMOVE in step 2).** Removing it is **blocked by `POAM-2026-183`** (reconciling the 3 live out-of-band `/32` CIDRs so [`staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh) does not strip `10.128.0.70/32` and `108.31.95.35/32`) |

GKE auto-upgraded the control plane 5 times from `"1.35.7-gke.1150000"` (`2026-09-06`) to `"1.35.8-gke.1796000"` (`2026-09-21`–`2026-10-27`). Furthermore, `hashicorp/google` `v6.50.0` **leaves `min_master_version = null` in state on `Read`** (populating `master_version = "1.35.8-gke.1796000"` instead), so removing `min_master_version` from `ignore_changes` plans `+ min_master_version = <configured>` on every apply and drifts after every GKE auto-upgrade | **Ordinary GKE `REGULAR` release-channel practice, NOT a security latch (KEEP in `ignore_changes`; removed from `POAM-2026-174` item 2).** The retired `"1.28"` create-time default that fails `CreateCluster` on a new project is tracked in `POAM-2026-164` (`EF10b`) |

3. **Live verification on `<PROJECT_ID>` (`2026-11-01` and `2026-11-04`, read-only `gcloud` inspection) and Correction (`2026-11-04`, `POAM-2026-169` instance 9):**
   - **Correction (`2026-11-04`):** When this entry was opened in `030b18d2` (`#637`, `2026-11-02`), its summary row and item 3 claimed `"verified unfired on <PROJECT_ID> (2026-11-01)"` and `"The defect is therefore unfired on <PROJECT_ID> today (because <PROJECT_ID> was applied via deploy_all.sh)"`. That claim was false: `gcloud container clusters describe` on `2026-11-01` observed only a point-in-time snapshot taken three days after manual `gcloud container clusters update` calls (`2026-10-28T19:18:17Z` and `2026-10-29T22:19:40Z`) had overwritten the cluster allowlist out-of-band, and used that snapshot to assert a historical claim ("unfired") without checking state history or Cloud Audit Logs. Both `gs://<PROJECT_ID>-tfstate/backups/cage-gcp-gke/default.tfstate.pre-migration-backup` (`serial: 689`) and Cloud Audit Logs (`2026-09-29T02:34:02Z`–`03:47:08Z`) show the latch **did** fire on `<PROJECT_ID>` and held `0.0.0.0/0` (`"All (Initial Staging Deployment)"`) for 29 days after commit `36b6bd60` (`#323`, `2026-09-29T17:31:28Z`) added `master_authorized_networks_config` to `ignore_changes` (recorded as `POAM-2026-169` instance 9).

   - `gcloud sql instances describe cage-postgres-staging --project=<PROJECT_ID>` confirmed `pointInTimeRecoveryEnabled = true`.
   - Comparing the live cluster's `masterAuthorizedNetworksConfig.cidrBlocks` against Terraform state and committed `.tfvars` files on `2026-11-04` showed that the live cluster (and `gs://<PROJECT_ID>-tfstate/cage/gcp-gke/cage-staging/default.tfstate`, `serial: 695`) holds three unattributed `/32` CIDRs (`136.115.30.143/32`, `10.128.0.70/32`, `108.31.95.35/32`) with `displayName = ""` applied out-of-band via `gcloud container clusters update` on `2026-10-28` and `2026-10-29`, tracked in **`POAM-2026-183`**. Reconciling `POAM-2026-183` is a prerequisite to removing `master_authorized_networks_config` from `ignore_changes` on `<PROJECT_ID>`.

**Remediation Plan:**
1. ✅ **Done (`2026-11-03`, pending merge):** step 1 set `enable_nist_compliance = true` in [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) (`line 92`), `POAM-2026-181` moved all controls to `local.enforcing_posture`, and `POAM-2026-130` removed the compliance flags so `-var-file=staging.tfvars` (`environment = "staging"`) plans the compliance posture whether invoked via [`deploy_all.sh`](../deploy_all.sh), [`scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh), or a direct `terraform plan`/`apply`/`destroy`.

Reconcile the live `<PROJECT_ID>` control-plane allowlist (`POAM-2026-183`), align `monitoring_config.enable_components` in [`infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) (`lines 256–259`, `EF10a` in `POAM-2026-164`) with live GKE 1.35 API components, and remove `master_authorized_networks_config` and `monitoring_config` from `lifecycle.ignore_changes` in [`infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gcp_gke_cluster/main.tf) (`lines 315, 299`), while keeping `node_version` and `min_master_version` (ordinary GKE `REGULAR` release-channel auto-upgrade attributes) and `database_encryption` (`POAM-2026-182` provider-enum workaround) in `ignore_changes`.
3. Add a regression test in [`tests/infrastructure/`](../tests/infrastructure/) asserting that `staging.tfvars` plans the enforcing posture directly and that `google_container_cluster.primary` does not ignore `master_authorized_networks_config` or `monitoring_config`. **Half done (`2026-11-03`):** `test_every_profile_declares_its_posture` and `test_enforcing_profiles_are_the_ones_terraform_hardens` in [`test_control_plane_access.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_control_plane_access.py) require every committed gcp-gke profile to declare `environment` and place `staging` in the enforcing set. Plan-time contracts are in [`staging_compliance.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/staging_compliance.tftest.hcl). The `master_authorized_networks_config` and `monitoring_config` `ignore_changes` half waits on step 2 (and `database_encryption` is guarded by [`test_gke_database_encryption_workaround.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_gke_database_encryption_workaround.py) under `POAM-2026-182`).
4. **Downstream dependency:** `POAM-2026-179` is waiting on step 2 so that `authorized_networks` changes take effect on an existing cluster instead of being masked by `ignore_changes`.

**What step 1 turned on in staging.** Every use of `any_compliance_active` in `main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf), and of the module input it fed, read at the step 1 commit. This changed only direct `terraform` runs; [`deploy_all.sh`](../deploy_all.sh) and [`staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh) (`lines 1097–1103`) already passed the flag.
