Four cfgs load and run under TLC; [`scripts/verify_tla.py`](../scripts/verify_tla.py) compares TLC output to the Python pins, `make verify-tla` runs it when `TLA_TOOLS_JAR` is set, and [`.github/workflows/tlc-model-check.yml`](../.github/workflows/tlc-model-check.yml) runs it on `workflow_dispatch` against a SHA-256-pinned `tla2tools.jar` v1.7.4.
3. [`tests/test_distributed_cbf_proof.py`](../tests/test_distributed_cbf_proof.py) pins `EXPECTED_STATE_COUNTS` for $N \in \{1,2,3\}$, the counterexamples, cfg/spec/Python constant parity, the `proof/model.py` counts (38/19/42), I-6 as `narrow_valid`, the verdict lattice, and `verdict_of()` parity against `ClassificationEngine`.
4. Published counts corrected in `proof/README.md`, `FORMAL_VERIFICATION.md`, `NON_FORMATION_PROOF_SPEC.md` and `REVISION_TRACKER.md`.

**Results (TLC v1.7.4 and BFS agree at $N = 2$, 2026-10-02):** the shipped posture (reconciled + `WAIT 1`) reaches 1,945 states with SP-1, SP-2 and SP-4 holding; `_nosync` (4,232) and `_selfreported` (1,933) violate SP-1 and SP-2; `_unfenced` (2,536) holds.

**Findings recorded by the remediation:**
- The plan's hypothesis that, without synchronous replication, "SP-1 holds iff no `AgentRestart` precedes `Replicate`" is **false**: a second process, or a rollback re-assigning `_last_seen_epoch` to the regressed epoch, re-admits the lost debit. `WAIT N` with strict rollback is the load-bearing defence.
- In reconciled mode the fence CAS is not needed for SP-1.
- Self-reported mode (refused by `CAGE_CBF_STRICT_MODE` under every enforcing posture) has a fence-epoch ABA and a rollback over-credit through `ROLLED_BACK_SETTLED`. This is a dev-only residual; no fix is in scope here. **Update 2026-10-03:** fixed in `e1a9687f` (#361): the self-reported script reads the live state key, a rollback without a ledger entry credits nothing (`ROLLED_BACK_UNLEDGERED`), and the `_selfreported` cfg was retired as identical to `DistributedCBF` (re-pinned counts: 1,811 / 3,972 / 2,388 at $N = 2$).
- Fixing the other cfgs so their constants load exposed POAM-2026-091.

**Closure Verification (2026-10-02):**
1. Squash-merged to `main` as `e3f1f7ff` (#351); `make test-fast` on the branch: 6034 passed, 100 skipped.
2. On `main` at `e3f1f7ff`: `TLA_TOOLS_JAR=… uv run python scripts/verify_tla.py` reported `DistributedCBF.cfg: TLC (states, SP-1, SP-2) = (1945, True, True); BFS = (1945, True, True)`, and all three negative-control cfgs matched their pins.
3. `uv run python -m src.gateway.governance.oscal_ssp_exporter export` succeeded; [`tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py`](../tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py) and [`tests/test_distributed_cbf_proof.py`](../tests/test_distributed_cbf_proof.py) passed (76 passed, 1 skipped: the live-TLC test, which needs `TLA_TOOLS_JAR`).

**Qualification — Python `WAIT` Realization vs. TLA+ `SyncReplication` (2026-10-31, `POAM-2026-156` / `fix/consume-once-durability`):**
Unlike `POAM-2026-154` (where `DistributedCBF.tla`'s `Read(a)` transition was coarser than the multi-`await` Python read), `DistributedCBF.tla`'s `SyncReplication` under `StaleFailover` was faithful in the formal model while [`cbf_engine.py`](../src/gateway/governance/safety/cbf_engine.py)'s prior Python realization of Redis `WAIT` had **two distinct code defects** that prevented the implementation from realizing `SyncReplication`:
1. **`rollback_state()` (unconditionally broken — not racy, needed no disconnect or adversary):** `rollback_state()` passed the pooled `target_client` (`self.redis_client`) into `_sync_to_replicas(client=target_client)` instead of pinning a single connection around `[LUA_ROLLBACK_CBF, WAIT]`. Because Redis `WAIT` acknowledges only writes issued on the **same connection**, `WAIT` could land on a different pooled connection from `LUA_ROLLBACK_CBF` on every call with no disconnect involved and return `>= 1` immediately without waiting for `LUA_ROLLBACK_CBF` to replicate.
2. **`atomic_verify_and_commit()` / `_sync_to_replicas()` (failover retry race):** even when `atomic_verify_and_commit()` pinned a `single_connection_client` via `self.redis_client.client()`, `_sync_to_replicas()` invoked `target_client.execute_command("WAIT", replicas, timeout)` through the retrying client. When the primary socket dropped between `LUA_ATOMIC_CBF` and `WAIT` — the exact primary failover `SyncReplication` exists to survive — `redis-py`'s `Retry` policy silently auto-reconnected on a fresh TCP socket with zero prior writes and re-sent `WAIT`, returning `>= 1` vacuously.
Both defects are remediated on `fix/consume-once-durability` via [`redis_replication.py`](../src/gateway/infrastructure/redis_replication.py) (`pinned_connection`, `write_socket`, `acknowledged_replicas`) and [`cbf_engine.py`](../src/gateway/governance/safety/cbf_engine.py), and verified by `test_cbf_rollback_state_runs_lua_and_wait_on_one_pinned_connection` (`M8`) and `test_cbf_commit_does_not_resend_wait_after_connection_drop` (`M9`) in [`tests/test_consequence_consumption_durability.py`](../tests/test_consequence_consumption_durability.py) (see `POAM-2026-156` mutation table).

**Correction — CI Evidence (2026-11-02):**
The closure verification above was local. Remediation item 2 gave [`tlc-model-check.yml`](../.github/workflows/tlc-model-check.yml) only a `workflow_dispatch` trigger, as approved-design item 1 specified, and the workflow was not dispatched until 2026-11-01, so the description's "TLC is not in CI" held after closure. The first run, `41362761903` (`main` at `2e6ffbac`), matched TLC to the BFS on all three `DistributedCBF` cfgs at $N = 2$: 1,811, 3,972 and 2,388 distinct states (summing to 8,171 over three state spaces, not one), with `_nosync` violating SP-1 and SP-2 and the other two holding. These are the counts #361 re-pinned; the 1,945 / 4,232 / 1,933 / 2,536 above are the four-cfg counts at `e3f1f7ff`. On `ci/connect-tlc-model-check` the workflow triggers on every pull request and every push to `main`, and [`tests/test_workflow_pr_triggers.py`](../tests/test_workflow_pr_triggers.py) fails if either trigger is removed. The closure date stays 2026-10-02, the date of the local verification.

**Correction — SP-4 Was Never Read from TLC (2026-11-02):**
[`verify_tla.py`](../scripts/verify_tla.py) parsed only `Invariant … is violated` lines, and for the `DistributedCBF` cfgs it discarded the rest of TLC's output. The cfgs declare `SP4_FenceEpochMonotonic` under `PROPERTIES`, and TLC reports a violation of it as `Action property SP4_FenceEpochMonotonic is violated.`, so every TLC run cited in this entry, local or CI, compared SP-1 and SP-2 against TLC but took SP-4 from the BFS. Under `-continue` TLC also exits 0 and prints `No error has been found` after reporting violations, so neither signal could have caught it. Reproduced on 2026-11-02 against the pinned v1.7.4 jar by planting SP-4's body false (`fence_epoch' = fence_epoch` in place of `>=`, a property-only change): TLC printed 768 such errors on `DistributedCBF.cfg` and 1,512 on `_nosync` with unchanged state counts, and the runner printed OK on all 8 cfgs. On `ci/connect-tlc-model-check` the runner fails on any TLC error line other than a pinned invariant violation or its trace header, and on any run that does not report completion. Against real TLC it reports BAD on all three `DistributedCBF` cfgs of the planted tree and OK on all 8 cfgs of the real one. `test_gate_tlc_model_check_rejects_unpinned_tlc_verdicts` in [`test_ci_gate_effectiveness.py`](../tests/test_ci_gate_effectiveness.py) plants the defect without Java, so it runs on every PR, and the runner from before the fix fails it.

### POAM-2026-091: FtraBoundary and LangGraphHarness TLA+ Specifications Have Never Been Model-Checked

**Control:** NIST CA-7 (Continuous Monitoring), SA-11 (Developer Testing and Evaluation)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Closed (2026-10-03)
**Date Opened:** 2026-10-02
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-13

**Description:**
While remediating POAM-2026-090, [`proof/FtraBoundary.cfg`](../proof/FtraBoundary.cfg) and [`proof/LangGraphHarness.cfg`](../proof/LangGraphHarness.cfg) were rewritten so their constants load. TLC then fails both specifications:
- [`proof/FtraBoundary.tla`](../proof/FtraBoundary.tla): the initial state violates `ControllerBoundaryCoversInGraphBypass`.
- [`proof/LangGraphHarness.tla`](../proof/LangGraphHarness.tla): several actions leave `consecutive_denials` and `deferral_resolved` unassigned, so successor states are undefined.

Neither specification had been model-checked before, so their stated invariants, including `SingleUseDeferralTicket` and `BudgetNeverExceededWithoutPause`, are unverified. `proof/README.md` marks both as "not model-checked".

**What was wrong beyond the reported symptoms:**
- *FtraBoundary:* the old spec claimed `market_analysis` was a registered `READ_ONLY` terminal (it is unregistered, so it classifies `IRREVERSIBLE_TERMINAL`), applied the 0.95 allow floor to `EMPTY_STEPS` plans (the node uses a literal 0.70), and stated the bypass invariant over every state, including the initial one. It modelled `bypassed_ftra_node` as a detection; in the code it is a telemetry label, because the controller cannot know whether the in-graph node ran.
- *LangGraphHarness:* the client-SDK session states (`Active`, `ParkedForReview`, `PausedBudgetExceeded`, `Completed`) were unreachable from `Init`. `SingleUseDeferralTicket` was a set tautology, and deferral resolution picked from `1..100`. The graph order was wrong (governance before FTRA), and an FTRA HITL resume could trade in the same turn; in the code it routes to the explainer.

**Remediation (as implemented):**
1. `FtraBoundary.tla` was rewritten as one request's lifecycle through the network policy, the in-graph `ftra_node` and the controller `FtraStage`, with the finance registry, independent registry loads per process, conditional auto-clear (registered terminal, envelope, magnitude inside it, confidence ≥ 0.95) and the in-graph verdict table. `ControllerBoundaryCoversInGraphBypass` is stated over governor phases. New invariants: `ControllerBoundaryUnconditional`, `NoUnreviewedIrreversibleExecution`, `RegistryUnavailableFailsClosed`, `AutoClearOnlyInsideEnvelope`, `HITLRequiredPropagates`, `ParseErrorsPreventClear`; liveness `EveryRequestTerminates` under weak fairness.
2. `LangGraphHarness.tla` was rewritten as one advisor thread over `MaxTurns` turns following `graph.py`, `safety_node.py` and the governed-trader subgraph, plus one DeferQueue ticket per turn (DEFER, FTRA and APPROVAL kinds) changing asynchronously. `BudgetNeverExceededWithoutPause` is checked at the real threshold: a refused turn reports `HARD_PAUSE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED` exactly when `consecutive_denials` ≥ 2. `SingleUseDeferralTicket` bounds each ticket to one resolution and one consumption. New invariants: `RefusedTurnNeverTrades`, `FtraHoldNeverTradesThisTurn`.
3. Negative controls `FtraBoundary_noboundary.cfg` (governor without `FtraStage`) and `LangGraphHarness_unguarded.cfg` (DeferQueue before POAM-2026-093); `FtraBoundary_nonetpol.cfg` shows the controller check alone suffices without the NetworkPolicy.
4. Results are pinned in [`proof/tla_pins.py`](../proof/tla_pins.py) and compared by [`scripts/verify_tla.py`](../scripts/verify_tla.py) (`make verify-tla`, [`.github/workflows/tlc-model-check.yml`](../.github/workflows/tlc-model-check.yml)). [`tests/test_tla_specs_proof.py`](../tests/test_tla_specs_proof.py) checks cfg/spec constant and invariant parity without Java and runs TLC when `TLA_TOOLS_JAR` is set.

**Results (TLC v1.7.4, `-continue`, 2026-10-03):**

| cfg | Distinct states | Result |
|-----|-----------------|--------|
| `FtraBoundary` (shipped; `FairSpec` + `EveryRequestTerminates`) | 8,272 | all invariants hold; liveness holds |
| `FtraBoundary_nonetpol` | 8,756 | all invariants hold |
| `FtraBoundary_noboundary` (negative control) | 13,792 | violates `NoUnreviewedIrreversibleExecution`, `ControllerBoundaryCoversInGraphBypass`, `ControllerBoundaryUnconditional` |
| `LangGraphHarness` (HEAD; 3 turns) | 82,652 | all invariants hold |
| `LangGraphHarness_unguarded` (negative control; 1 turn) | 906 | violates `SingleUseDeferralTicket` |

**Findings recorded by the remediation:**
- `SingleUseDeferralTicket` failed against the code: tracked and fixed as POAM-2026-093.
- The POAM-2026-024 verification claim was vacuous; a dated note on that row records it.
- The consecutive-denial pause ends the turn; it does not lock the thread, and the counter persists in the thread checkpoint. [`cage_client_sdk.yaml`](../compliance/oscal/components/cage_client_sdk.yaml) was corrected accordingly.
- `loop_count` is never reset within a thread, so after three rejected plans every later plan on that thread goes straight to the explainer.
- `ConsistentClassification` holds when both processes load the registry the same way; when only one load fails, the two classifications differ, but both fail closed.
- The NetworkPolicy remains an interim compensating control; the shipped posture does not depend on it (`_nonetpol`).

**Closure Verification (2026-10-03):**
1. `JAVA=… TLA_TOOLS_JAR=… uv run python scripts/verify_tla.py` on the branch reported `OK` for all eight cfgs (three `DistributedCBF`, three `FtraBoundary`, two `LangGraphHarness`), with the counts above.
2. `make test-fast` on the branch: 6080 passed, 105 skipped. One failure, `tests/test_gfa_nodes_coverage.py::TestExecutionAnalystNodeBehavior::test_circuit_breaker_fires_at_loop_count_3`, is order-dependent and pre-existing: it fails identically at the merge base `7d359c80` (6054 passed, 100 skipped, 1 failed) and passes in isolation.
3. `uv run python -m src.gateway.governance.oscal_ssp_exporter export` succeeded; [`tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py`](../tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py) passed (50 passed).
4. Squash-merged to `main` as #380.

**Correction — CI Evidence (2026-11-02):**
Item 1 above was a local run. Remediation item 4 cites [`tlc-model-check.yml`](../.github/workflows/tlc-model-check.yml) as running the comparison, but the workflow had only a `workflow_dispatch` trigger and was not dispatched until 2026-11-01. The first run, `41362761903` (`main` at `2e6ffbac`), reproduced every row of the results table: 8,272, 8,756, 13,792, 82,652 and 906 distinct states, with `FtraBoundary_noboundary` violating exactly `NoUnreviewedIrreversibleExecution`, `ControllerBoundaryCoversInGraphBypass` and `ControllerBoundaryUnconditional`, and `LangGraphHarness_unguarded` violating exactly `SingleUseDeferralTicket`. On `ci/connect-tlc-model-check` the workflow triggers on every pull request and every push to `main`. The runner gap recorded under POAM-2026-090 did not affect these results. These cfgs declare no action property, and the old runner already failed them on a temporal-property violation (`EveryRequestTerminates`); the stricter runner reports OK on all five against TLC v1.7.4. The closure date stays 2026-10-03, the date of the local verification.

### POAM-2026-092: Reconciled CBF Settles Debits the Custodian Does Not Yet Carry

**Control:** NIST SI-10 (Information Input Validation), `CTRL_MRM_004`
**Risk Level:** High
**Status:** Closed (2026-10-03)
**Date Opened:** 2026-10-03
**Target Closure:** 2026-10-17

**Description:**
The settlement-aware ledger (POAM-2026-087, ADR-010) stamped each debit's `submitted_at` when the CBF committed it, which is before the actuator tells the custodian about the fill. The reconciler settles debits stamped at or before `min(settled_through, verified_at) − settlement_clock_skew_seconds`. If the commit-to-custodian latency exceeded the 5 s margin, a debit could be settled while the snapshot did not yet carry it, and the admissible headroom was overstated by that debit until the custodian caught up. The discrepancy guard only catches trades that are large relative to the balance. POAM-2026-087 recorded this as a residual limitation.

**Remediation (implemented):**
1. [`debit_ledger.py`](../src/gateway/governance/safety/debit_ledger.py): a commit enters `cbf:debits:pending`. `LUA_CONFIRM_DEBIT` moves it to `cbf:debits:by_time` stamped with the confirm time. `LUA_SETTLE_DEBITS` settles only `by_time` and promotes pending entries older than `reconciliation.pending_debit_max_age_seconds` (default 600 s, at least the governor's settlement hold) stamped at promotion. `LUA_UNSETTLED_TOTAL` counts every pending debit.
2. [`cbf_engine.py`](../src/gateway/governance/safety/cbf_engine.py): the commit script writes the pending set; rollback clears both sets; new `ControlBarrierFunction.confirm_debit()`.
3. [`barrier_tier.py`](../src/gateway/governance/safety/barrier_tier.py): `confirm_barrier()`; the finance, healthcare and physical-AI barrier tiers call it from ADR-009 `confirm()`, which the governor runs only after the action executed. The multi-engine kinematic tier now keeps each engine's receipt, so rollback and confirm address each engine's own `debit_id` (rollback previously restored by magnitude only and left the ledger entries in place).
4. Tests: `test_unconfirmed_debit_is_never_settled_however_late_the_actuator_runs`, `test_unconfirmed_debit_is_promoted_after_the_max_age`, `test_settle_never_prunes_pending_debits`, `test_pending_max_age_outlasts_the_governor_settlement_hold` ([`tests/test_cbf_settlement_ledger.py`](../tests/test_cbf_settlement_ledger.py)); tier wiring in [`tests/governor/test_commit_receipts.py`](../tests/governor/test_commit_receipts.py).

**Closure Verification (2026-10-03):**
1. Squash-merged to `main` as `42ee97a5` (#359); `make test-fast` on the branch: 6037 passed, 100 skipped.
2. `make test-fast` on `main` at `e1a9687f`: 6037 passed, 100 skipped.
3. `uv run python -m src.gateway.governance.oscal_ssp_exporter export` succeeded; [`tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py`](../tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py) passed (50 passed).

### POAM-2026-093: DeferQueue Tokens Could Be Resolved More Than Once

**Control:** NIST AC-3 (Access Enforcement), SI-10 (Information Input Validation)
**Risk Level:** High
**Status:** Closed (2026-10-03)
**Date Opened:** 2026-10-03
**Target Closure:** 2026-10-03

**Description:**
Found while model-checking `SingleUseDeferralTicket` for POAM-2026-091. [`DeferQueue._resolve`](../src/gateway/governance/defer_queue.py) and `replay_evaluate` checked only the token revision in their CAS, never the token status:
- `POST /v1/defer/{id}/inject` on the compliance bridge ([`main.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/main.py)) re-resolved a token that was already `INJECTED`, already `CONSUMED`, or quorum-approved (`ESCALATED`). The bridge's reason gates reject quorum-3 tokens and partial approvals, but a fully approved 2/2 `HITL_REQUIRED` token passed them.
- `expire_stale` could overwrite a quorum approval with `EXPIRED` in the window between `approve()`'s CAS and its `zrem` from the expiry index.

The approval itself could not be spent twice: `consume_approval` requires `ESCALATED`, and `RESOLVED → CONSUMED` is an atomic CAS. The defect let the resolution record, and the audit trail built from it, be rewritten after the fact.

**Remediation (implemented):**
1. `_RESOLVABLE_FROM` in [`defer_queue.py`](../src/gateway/governance/defer_queue.py): `INJECTED` only from `PARKED`; `EXPIRED` and `ESCALATED` only from `PARKED` or `PARTIALLY_APPROVED`. `_resolve` raises on an unknown resolution and refuses (logs, drops the expiry-index entry, returns `None`) any other status.
2. `replay_evaluate` reads the status with the revision and returns the new `ReplayResult.ALREADY_RESOLVED` for any token not `PARKED` or when `_resolve` refuses.
3. `defer_inject` maps `ALREADY_RESOLVED` to HTTP 409 `DEFER_TOKEN_ALREADY_RESOLVED` (previously a 500 path).
4. [`tests/test_defer_queue_single_use.py`](../tests/test_defer_queue_single_use.py): re-inject after `INJECTED`, inject after quorum approval, after `CONSUMED` and after a partial approval, the `expire_stale`-vs-approve race at the guard, expiry of parked and partially approved tokens still working, unknown resolution, and two endpoint 409 tests. Eight of the nine fail without the fix.

**Closure Verification (2026-10-03):**
1. `LangGraphHarness.cfg` (guarded `_resolve`) holds `SingleUseDeferralTicket` over 82,652 states; `LangGraphHarness_unguarded.cfg` (the code before the fix) violates it.
2. `make test-fast` on the branch: 6080 passed, 105 skipped. One failure, `tests/test_gfa_nodes_coverage.py::TestExecutionAnalystNodeBehavior::test_circuit_breaker_fires_at_loop_count_3`, is order-dependent and pre-existing: it fails identically at the merge base `7d359c80` (6054 passed, 100 skipped, 1 failed) and passes in isolation.
3. Squash-merged to `main` as #380.

### POAM-2026-095 to POAM-2026-098: Findings From Verifying the Paper Against the Code

**Date Opened:** 2026-10-03
**Source:** checking the arXiv manuscript's claims against the code at `fcfb4ebb`.

| ID | Control | Risk Level | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| POAM-2026-095 | SC-13 / SC-12 / IA-5 | High | Closed (2026-10-03) |
| POAM-2026-096 | CA-7 / SI-10 | High | Open (Reopened `2026-11-05` — **Out-of-state closure**, `F-50`, `F-86`; see detail section below) |
| POAM-2026-097 | SI-10 / SC-23 | High | Closed (2026-10-03) |
| POAM-2026-098 | AU-10 / AC-3 / SI-10 | Moderate | Open — defect in CAGE `verify_partner_receipt()` (target 2026-12-31) |

**Description:**
- **095:** no asymmetric routing seal could verify. The `alg` header held a provider label, and ECDSA signatures were left in DER form instead of JOSE raw R‖S. A seal with no `kid` fell back to the single published key.
- **096:** the reconciler's 5-minute CronJob cadence equalled the 300 s verified-state TTL, so verified state lapsed between runs. The discrepancy-floor branch also wrote the CBF state key from a reading it had just rejected.
- **097:** the ground-truth replay check was a non-atomic read-then-write, and a Redis error let it pass.
- **098:** actuation outcomes were binary, so an indeterminate dispatch released reserved capacity and could be retried (fail-open). Partner receipts were trusted without authentication. Three call sites each wrote the receipt to the evidence chain. A hidden attribute set on the frozen receipt suppressed the duplicate writes, so no receipt was recorded twice, but the evidence had two owners and depended on hidden mutable state.

**Remediation (implemented):**
1. **095** (`9c68a6e8`): `generate_seal()` in [`routing_seal.py`](../src/gateway/governance/routing_seal.py) writes `jose_alg` to the header. `KMSGovernanceSigner.sign_jws()` in [`kms_signer.py`](../src/gateway/governance/kms_signer.py) converts DER to raw, and providers declare their native encoding. [`jwks.py`](../src/gateway/governance/jwks.py) requires a `kid`.
2. **096** (`9c68a6e8`): the reconciler runs as a Deployment in loop mode, and `run_loop()` enforces `ttl >= 2 × poll`. The floor branch never writes state.
3. **097** (`9c68a6e8`): an atomic Lua compare-and-advance in [`cbf_engine.py`](../src/gateway/governance/safety/cbf_engine.py) that fails closed on a Redis error.
4. **098** (`9c68a6e8`, `72574529`):
   - `ActuationOutcome` {ACCEPTED, REJECTED, UNKNOWN}. `UNKNOWN` settles as executed and is never retryable.
   - `dispatch_actuation()` is the sole caller of `actuate()` and the sole evidence writer. An actuator exception becomes an `UNKNOWN` receipt (`ACTUATOR_EXCEPTION`).
   - `ReceiptVerification` is recorded with every receipt.
   - actuator_01 verifies a detached Ed25519 signature by `kid`, using the shared [`Ed25519KeyManifestClient`](../src/integrations/trust/key_manifest.py) (`INVALID` forces `UNKNOWN`). Strict mode is `ACTUATOR_01_REQUIRE_SIGNED_RECEIPTS`.
   - Evidence: [`tests/test_actuation_outcome.py`](../tests/test_actuation_outcome.py), [`tests/test_actuator_01_receipt_verification.py`](../tests/test_actuator_01_receipt_verification.py).

**Closure Verification (2026-10-03):**
1. `make test-fast` on `fix/paper-verify-findings` at `72574529`: 6203 passed, 107 skipped, 0 failed.
2. `uv run python -m src.gateway.governance.oscal_ssp_exporter export` succeeded. [`tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py`](../tests/test_oscal_ssp_exporter.py): 50 passed. The OSCAL statements are in [`sp800-53-component-definition.yaml`](../compliance/oscal/sp800-53-component-definition.yaml) with `poam-reference` props. SC-13/095, CA-7/096 and SI-10/097 are `implemented`. AU-10/098 is `partial`.
3. Lula: no assertion in `compliance/lula/` references the reconciliation worker, routing-seal algorithms or actuation receipts, so no Lula result is affected.
4. Squash-merge commit: `e1f46eca` (#391).

**Open for 098 (attribution corrected 2026-11-02):** Earlier text in this entry (`#391`, `e1f46eca`) misattributed the open verification gap to the `actuator_01` partner (Archytan), stating that the partner had not yet adopted receipt signing. In fact, Archytan's kernel signs actuation receipts with Ed25519 (`kid = "archytan-sandbox-receipt-2026-01"` published at `GET /v1/keys/receipt-manifest`), and the defect is in CAGE's [`verify_partner_receipt()`](../src/integrations/actuator_01/receipt_verifier.py) (`lines 193–234`) and [`src/integrations/actuator_01/README.md`](../src/integrations/actuator_01/README.md) (`lines 164–188`): (1) `verify_partner_receipt()` constructs the signed preimage as `domain_tag + jcs_canonicalize_plan(candidate)` (`b"ARCHYTAN_RECEIPT_V1:"` or legacy `b"CAGE_ACTUATION_RECEIPT_V1:"` concatenated with RFC 8785 JCS of the receipt body without `"signature"` and optionally without `"ingress_signature"`, `lines 193–223`), whereas Archytan's kernel signs `"ARCHYTAN_RECEIPT_V1:" || SHA-256(intent_hash)`; and (2) `verify_partner_receipt()` requires `verified_body.get("envelope_digest") == envelope_digest` (`lines 230–234`), whereas Archytan receipts do not carry an `envelope_digest` field (they carry `intent_hash`, the full JCS-canonicalized governance envelope, alongside `receipt_id`, `timestamp_ns`, `status`, `signature`, and `ingress_signature`, and bind to the envelope via `SHA-256(intent_hash)` in the signed preimage). Because the `2026-09-17` joint sandbox integration session was postponed on `2026-09-15` (and [`ci-partner-integration.yml`](../.github/workflows/ci-partner-integration.yml) still has not run `actuator_01` against the sandbox: prior to `#598`, `43528275`, `2026-10-30`, the workflow had never been dispatched and skipped every `live_external` test, and in run `41125558418` on `#598` all 5 `actuator_01` tests skipped and the harness failed closed because `ACTUATOR_01_ENDPOINT`, `ACTUATOR_01_TENANT_ID`, and the mTLS PEM secrets are not configured in CI; `POAM-2026-150`), this path had never run against Archytan's sandbox, which is why the mismatch went unobserved. Luis Fernando Martinez Chavez reported both mismatches twice (`2026-10-15` and `2026-10-23`). PR `#534` (`42b819d4`, `2026-10-24`) updated [`constants.py`](../src/integrations/actuator_01/constants.py) (`RECEIPT_SIGNATURE_DOMAIN_TAG = b"ARCHYTAN_RECEIPT_V1:"`), added flat string `signature` extraction and `ingress_signature` stripping in [`receipt_verifier.py`](../src/integrations/actuator_01/receipt_verifier.py), and removed the `(CAGE-proposed extension)` heading in [`src/integrations/actuator_01/README.md`](../src/integrations/actuator_01/README.md), but kept `domain_tag + jcs_canonicalize_plan(candidate)` and `verified_body.get("envelope_digest")`, so it did not resolve the defect. Closure requires fixing `verify_partner_receipt()` (and [`src/integrations/actuator_01/README.md`](../src/integrations/actuator_01/README.md)) against a captured receipt fixture from Archytan's kernel, passing an over-the-wire conformance test against the Archytan sandbox, and enabling strict mode (`ACTUATOR_01_REQUIRE_SIGNED_RECEIPTS`) in enforcing postures. `Provider07JwksClient` should move to the shared manifest client.

### POAM-2026-096: Reconciliation Worker Simulated Provider Hardcoded and Unchained Discrepancy Fences (Reopened — Out-of-State Closure; IaC Merged in `#664`)

**Control:** NIST CA-7 (Continuous Monitoring), SI-10 (Information Input Validation)
**Risk Level:** High
**Status:** Open (Reopened `2026-11-05` — **Out-of-state closure**, `F-50`, `F-86`; original `2026-10-03` closure withdrawn; Terraform module [`infra/modules/reconciliation_worker`](../infra/modules/reconciliation_worker/main.tf) and `cage-reconciler-sa` Workload Identity merged on `main` in `#664` `372daad2` on `2026-11-06`)
**Date Opened:** 2026-10-03 (reopened `2026-11-05`)
**Target Closure:** 2026-12-31

**Why the `2026-10-03` Closure Is Withdrawn:**
1. **`reconciliation-worker` was not in Terraform state at closure (`F-50`; remediated in `#664` `372daad2`):** `POAM-2026-096` was closed on `2026-10-03` (`9c68a6e8`, `#391` `e1f46eca`) citing `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/reconciliation-worker.yaml` and [`src/gateway/governance/reconciliation/daemon.py`](../src/gateway/governance/reconciliation/daemon.py) while no Terraform resource applied that manifest and `infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf` commented out `cage-reconciler-sa`'s Workload Identity binding (`default.tfstate` `serial: 695`, `F-47`). PR `#664` (`372daad2`, `2026-11-06`) added [`infra/modules/reconciliation_worker`](../infra/modules/reconciliation_worker/main.tf), instantiated `module.reconciliation_worker` in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf), and bound `cage-reconciler-sa` in [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf), pending live clean-walk verification (`W`).
2. **`ReconciliationDaemon.from_env()` hardcodes `_DefaultSimulatedProvider` (`F-50`):** [`src/gateway/governance/reconciliation/daemon.py`](../src/gateway/governance/reconciliation/daemon.py) carries `# TODO(v3.1): Wire production external ledger provider here` and unconditionally instantiates `_DefaultSimulatedProvider()`, which returns a constant `100_000.0` balance while [`sp800-53-component-definition.yaml`](../compliance/oscal/sp800-53-component-definition.yaml) marks `CA-7` and `SI-10` `implemented`.
3. **Discrepancy fence emits no signed, hash-chained `EvidenceRecord` (`F-86`):** When `ReconciliationDaemon.reconcile_once()` detects a balance discrepancy exceeding `max(ratio * |baseline|, floor)` in [`src/gateway/governance/reconciliation/daemon.py`](../src/gateway/governance/reconciliation/daemon.py), it sets an OTel span attribute (`cage.reconciliation.discrepancy_detected = True`), increments a counter, and deletes `reconciliation:verified_balance` in Redis, without appending a signed, hash-chained `RECONCILIATION_DISCREPANCY_FENCE` `EvidenceRecord` to `EvidenceStreamSink` in Redis DB 1.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. ✅ **Done in IaC (`#664`, `372daad2`, `2026-11-06`):** [`infra/modules/reconciliation_worker`](../infra/modules/reconciliation_worker/main.tf) and `cage-reconciler-sa` Workload Identity are managed in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/`.
2. `PR-7c` restates the Tier 2 simulated-ledger boundary (`_DefaultSimulatedProvider`) in [`sp800-53-component-definition.yaml`](../compliance/oscal/sp800-53-component-definition.yaml) and [`oscal_ssp_exporter.py`](../src/gateway/governance/oscal_ssp_exporter.py) (`CA-7` / `SI-10`), and `§11b` (`R-7`) emits a signed, hash-chained `RECONCILIATION_DISCREPANCY_FENCE` `EvidenceRecord` via `EvidenceStreamSink` on every discrepancy fence event before invalidating the Redis key.
3. Clean-walk verification (`W`) confirms `reconciliation-worker` is provisioned by Terraform and publishes KMS-signed snapshots to governance Valkey.

### POAM-2026-189: Broken Default `gcp_geap_advisor` Vertex AI `ReasoningEngine` Module and Duplicate Advisor Runtime (`F-21`–`F-26`)

**Control:** NIST CM-6 (Configuration Settings), SA-9 (External System Services), SC-7 (Boundary Protection), SC-28 (Protection of Information at Rest)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open — verified in `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`, `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf`, and `385e502e:infra/modules/gcp_geap_advisor/main.tf` on `2026-11-05`; remediated on `fix/retire-geap-runtime` (`AR-1`), pending merge SHA
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **Default `advisor_runtime = "geap"` vs. `staging.tfvars` (`F-21`):** `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf` defaulted `advisor_runtime = "geap"`, while `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars` overrode `advisor_runtime = "gke"`, leaving `module.gcp_geap_advisor` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`) uninstantiated in the maintained staging profile while remaining the default in `variables.tf`.
2. **Unwired VPC/CMEK inputs and unreachable cluster-local DNS on `ReasoningEngine` (`F-22`–`F-24`):** `385e502e:infra/modules/gcp_geap_advisor/main.tf` deployed a Vertex AI Agent Engine (`ReasoningEngine`) via an inline `gcloud` / Python `local-exec` provisioner on `terraform_data.geap_reasoning_engine`. `vpc_network`, `vpc_subnetwork`, and `kms_key_name` appear only inside `triggers_replace` (`lines 115–117`) and are omitted from the `ReasoningEngine` deployment call, while `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) passes `vpc_network = var.network` (`"default"`) and cluster-local `.svc.cluster.local` URLs (`http://gateway-service.governance-stack.svc.cluster.local:8080`, `http://vllm-service.governance-stack.svc.cluster.local:8000/v1`, `redis_host = "redis.governance-stack.svc.cluster.local"`) that Vertex AI Agent Engine cannot resolve (`POAM-2026-135`).
3. **`terraform_data` state-orphaning hazard and architectural duplication (`F-26`, `AR-1`):** Deleting `module.gcp_geap_advisor` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`) while any workspace state still holds `terraform_data.geap_reasoning_engine` (`385e502e:infra/modules/gcp_geap_advisor/main.tf`) requires manual `gcloud ai reasoning-engines delete` cleanup if a live `ReasoningEngine` was ever provisioned (`F-26`, documented in `AR-1`; `default.tfstate` `serial: 695` holds zero instances because `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars` set `advisor_runtime = "gke"`), and maintaining a second `local-exec` advisor deployment path alongside the GKE [`infra/modules/governed_advisor/`](../infra/modules/governed_advisor/main.tf) pod violates the single-target GKE reference architecture established in `POAM-2026-185`.

**Remediation Plan (`AR-1`, `PR-3`):**
1. Delete `385e502e:infra/modules/gcp_geap_advisor/main.tf`, `385e502e:src/integrations/gcp_geap/runtime_guards.py`, `385e502e:src/gateway/governance/ingress/agw_adapter.py`, `385e502e:docs/architecture/GEAP_INTEGRATION_ARCHITECTURE.md`, and their dedicated unit/infrastructure tests ( while extracting neutral out-of-band `kid`-resolved OIDC verification to [`src/compliance_bridge/oidc_verifier.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/oidc_verifier.py) and removing `CAGE_TRUSTED_GEAP_AGENT_PRINCIPALS` from [`workload_identity.py`](../src/gateway/server/workload_identity.py), `F-113`).
2. Remove `module "gcp_geap_advisor"` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf`) and `variable "advisor_runtime"` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf`) from `infra/targets/gcp-gke/` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) so `module.governed_advisor` is unconditionally the sole advisor runtime on GKE.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `385e502e:infra/modules/gcp_geap_advisor/main.tf`, `385e502e:src/integrations/gcp_geap/runtime_guards.py`, `385e502e:src/gateway/governance/ingress/agw_adapter.py`, `385e502e:docs/architecture/GEAP_INTEGRATION_ARCHITECTURE.md`, and `advisor_runtime` (`385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf`) are absent from the repository (`PR-3`), `terraform validate` and `terraform test` pass in `infra/targets/gcp-gke`, and the merge commit SHA is recorded here.

### POAM-2026-190: Static Data-Store Egress CIDRs Matching No Allocated Endpoint, Copied `default`-VPC Ranges, and Missing Memorystore PSC Policy (`F-10`, `F-11`, `F-14`, `F-15`)

**Control:** NIST SC-7 (Boundary Protection), CM-6 (Configuration Settings)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open (IaC Remediated in `PR-1` `#681` `1f8641f6` + `PR-2` / `CP-6`) — `PR-1` (`#681`, `1f8641f6`) remediated endpoint-derived `/32` data-store CIDRs, `CONNECTION_TYPE_PRIMARY` output selection, static `*_cidr` removal, `local.cloud_sql_cidr` hash inclusion (`F-15`), and `the monolithic datastore egress policy`](49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) separation, and `PR-2` (`CP-6`, `POAM-2026-173`) provisioned `google_network_connectivity_service_connection_policy.memorystore` (`service_class = "gcp-memorystore"`) in [`infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf`](../infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf) and bound GKE, Linkerd webhook firewall, Cloud SQL, and both Memorystore Valkey instances to `module.vpc_network`; pending live staging verification (`W`)
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **Static data-store egress CIDRs matched no allocated endpoint (`F-11`, `F-15`, remediated in `PR-1` `#681`):** `c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf` (`lines 1126–1142`) defined static CIDR variables (`memorystore_governance_psc_cidr`, `memorystore_app_psc_cidr`, `cloud_sql_cidr`) consumed by `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) that matched no live Memorystore PSC or Cloud SQL Private Service Access IP; connectivity worked only because `default_deny_external_egress` in `network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) masked them with a broad `10.0.0.0/8` rule (`POAM-2026-195`). Meanwhile `c58b0913:infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/outputs.tf` indexed `endpoints[0].connections[0]` by position instead of selecting `connection_type == "CONNECTION_TYPE_PRIMARY"` (`F-14`), and `local.network_policy_spec_hash` omitted `cloud_sql_cidr` (`F-15`).
2. **Copied `default`-VPC CIDRs in `staging.tfvars` (`F-10`, remediated in `PR-1` `#681`):** `c58b0913:infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars` (`lines 225–227`) set `memorystore_governance_psc_cidr = "10.128.0.0/20"`, `memorystore_app_psc_cidr = "10.128.0.0/20"`, and `cloud_sql_cidr = "10.6.80.0/20"`, duplicating the `default` VPC's `us-central1` subnet and out-of-band PSA ranges (`POAM-2026-173` Finding C).
3. **Missing Memorystore PSC service connection policy (`EF7c`, remediated in `PR-2` / `CP-6`):** `c58b0913:infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf` allocated only the Cloud SQL Private Service Access (`google_compute_global_address.private_ip_alloc`) range without provisioning `google_network_connectivity_service_connection_policy.memorystore` (`service_class = "gcp-memorystore"`). Note on scope: [`infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf`](../infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf) already configures `INCLUDE_ALL_METADATA` firewall logging and Cloud NAT `filter = "ALL"` when `enable_audit_logging = true`, and per-workload Kubernetes pod-to-datastore isolation (`5432` / `6379`) is tracked under `POAM-2026-195` (`PR-7b`).

**Remediation (`PR-1` `#681` + `PR-2` IaC complete; live `W` verification pending):**
1. **`PR-1` (`#681`, `1f8641f6`, `DE-1` in [`BREAKING_CHANGES.md`](BREAKING_CHANGES.md)):** Deleted `memorystore_governance_psc_cidr`, `memorystore_app_psc_cidr`, and `cloud_sql_cidr` from [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf) and [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars); filtered `primary_endpoint_ip` and `primary_endpoint_port` on `connection_type == "CONNECTION_TYPE_PRIMARY"` in [`infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/outputs.tf`](../infra/modules/memorystore_valkey/outputs.tf) ([`governance_persistence.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/governance_persistence.tftest.hcl)); derived `/32` endpoint locals (`local.governance_valkey_cidr`, `local.app_valkey_cidr`, `local.cloud_sql_cidr`) in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf), included `local.cloud_sql_cidr` in `local.network_policy_spec_hash`, and separated namespace-wide data-store allow rules into `the monolithic datastore egress policy`](49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) ([`w6.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/w6.tftest.hcl), [`test_evidence_custody_wiring.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_evidence_custody_wiring.py)).
2. **`PR-2` (`POAM-2026-173` / `CP-6` in [`BREAKING_CHANGES.md`](BREAKING_CHANGES.md)):** Provisioned `google_network_connectivity_service_connection_policy.memorystore` (`service_class = "gcp-memorystore"`) in [`infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf`](../infra/modules/vpc_network/main.tf) and bound GKE, Linkerd webhook firewall, Cloud SQL, and both Memorystore Valkey instances to `module.vpc_network` ([`vpc_binding.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/vpc_binding.tftest.hcl), [`test_memorystore_topology.py`](../tests/infrastructure/test_memorystore_topology.py)).

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `PR-1` and `PR-2` merge with `terraform test` and infrastructure unit tests pinning `CONNECTION_TYPE_PRIMARY` output selection, dynamic `/32` endpoint CIDRs, custom-VPC binding, and the Memorystore service connection policy.
2. Clean-walk deployment (`W`, `§W`) verifies Memorystore PSC attachment, `/32` NetworkPolicy egress, and active VPC firewall & Cloud NAT log entries in Cloud Logging (`R-5`, `F-87`).

### POAM-2026-191: Governed Advisor Uninstantiated on Staging, Direct `vllm-service` Bypass, and Missing CPU Stub E2E Conformance (`F-29`–`F-36`, `F-103`, `R-16`)

**Control:** NIST CA-7 (Continuous Monitoring), SA-11 (Developer Testing and Evaluation), SC-7 (Boundary Protection), SI-10 (Information Input Validation)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open — verified in [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars), `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf), `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf), and [`infra/modules/governed_advisor/main.tf`](../infra/modules/governed_advisor/main.tf) on `2026-11-05`
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **Governed advisor uninstantiated under `staging.tfvars` (`F-29`–`F-32`):** [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars) sets `inference_backend = "none"` (to avoid GPU node pools on ephemeral staging clusters), which disables `module.governed_advisor` (`count = 0` in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf)), so no governed advisor pod runs on staging and end-to-end advisor → gateway tool-execution governance (`ALLOW`, `DENY`, `DEFER`, `POST_HITL`) is untested on live clusters.
2. **Direct advisor → `vllm-service:8000` model routing and missing inference ingress `NetworkPolicy` (`EA-1`, `F-33`–`F-36`):** `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) configures `module.governed_advisor` to send chat-completion calls directly to `http://vllm-service.governance-stack.svc.cluster.local:8000/v1` instead of routing through the gateway's OpenAI-compatible inference proxy (`POST /v1/chat/completions` in [`src/gateway/server/inference_proxy.py`](../src/gateway/server/inference_proxy.py)), and `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) lacks an ingress `NetworkPolicy` restricting TCP `8000` on inference pods exclusively to `app = gateway`.
3. **No CPU-only `inference_backend = "stub"` for deterministic E2E staging conformance (`F-103`, `R-16`):** No deterministic OpenAI-compatible CPU stub server exists to exercise the full `governed-advisor` → `gateway` (`POST /v1/chat/completions` + `/tools/execute`) → `DeferQueue` loop on staging without GPUs while keeping LLM semantic rails (`CAGE_CONSENSUS_CRITICS_ENABLED`, `CAGE_NEMO_ENABLED`) disabled and refusing `inference_backend = "stub"` in `environment == "prod"`.

**Remediation Plan (`PR-6`):**
1. Add a deterministic OpenAI-compatible CPU stub backend (`inference_backend = "stub"`, refused in `prod` via a `lifecycle` `precondition`), re-route `module.governed_advisor` model traffic through `http://gateway-service.<ns>.svc.cluster.local:8080/v1`, add an ingress `NetworkPolicy` restricting TCP `8000` on inference pods to `app = gateway`, and add an E2E advisor proxy conformance suite exercising `ALLOW`, `DENY`, `DEFER`, and `POST_HITL` without GPUs (`R-16`).

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `PR-6` merges with `terraform test` (verifying `inference_backend = "stub"` works in `staging` and fails plan in `prod`), ingress `NetworkPolicy` isolation on TCP `8000`, and green E2E advisor proxy conformance tests (`ALLOW`/`DENY`/`DEFER`/`POST_HITL`).
2. Clean-walk deployment (`W`) runs the E2E advisor proxy conformance suite against the live staging cluster.

### POAM-2026-192: Orphaned `agentsight` and `cage-eval-worker` Workloads and Pending ClickHouse Pod on Live `cage-staging` (`F-47`, `F-48`)

**Control:** NIST CM-2 (Baseline Configuration), CM-6 (Configuration Settings)
**Risk Level:** Low
**Status:** Open — verified live on `<PROJECT_ID>` (`cage-staging`) and against `gs://<PROJECT_ID>-tfstate/cage/gcp-gke/cage-staging/default.tfstate` (`serial: 695`) on `2026-11-05` (`reconciliation-worker` IaC landed in `#664` `372daad2` on `2026-11-06`)
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **Workloads running on `cage-staging` outside `infra/targets/gcp-gke` Terraform state (`F-47`):** Comparing `kubectl get deployments,daemonsets,statefulsets -A` on `cage-staging` against `default.tfstate` (`serial: 695`) identified workloads running in `governance-stack` outside `infra/targets/gcp-gke`: the `agentsight` DaemonSet (`de5534ac:deployment/k8s/agentsight-daemon.yaml`), `cage-eval-worker`, and `reconciliation-worker` (`#664` `372daad2` subsequently added [`infra/modules/reconciliation_worker`](../infra/modules/reconciliation_worker/main.tf) to Terraform on `2026-11-06`).
2. **Unschedulable ClickHouse pod on `cage-staging` (`F-48`):** `chi-cage-clickhouse-cage-0-0-0` sits in `Pending` (`0/1`) on `cage-staging` (`default.tfstate` `serial: 695`) because `clickhouse_node_count = 0` in [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars), while `agentsight-ui` is configured with `CLICKHOUSE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8123"` (`F-49`). Note: ClickHouse is retained as Langfuse v3's OLAP backend (`#663` `24a10b14` isolated the `default` superuser and scoped `langfuse_app` to `langfuse.*`, and `#667` `4c48d107` retired the uninstantiated `4c48d107^:src/compliance_bridge/clickhouse_sink.py` governance sink).

**Remediation Plan:**
1. Retire AgentSight under `§11a` (`R-3`, `POAM-2026-193`), size/schedule Langfuse's ClickHouse backend cleanly for staging, and replace the cluster from clean Terraform state during `W` (`§W`), recording the removal of the unmanaged `agentsight` and `cage-eval-worker` workloads as intended disposal.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. Clean-walk deployment (`W`, `§W`) shows zero workloads in `governance-stack` outside `default.tfstate` and zero `Pending` workload pods after Stage 2 apply.

### POAM-2026-193: Dormant AgentSight Infrastructure Carrying Privileged DaemonSet, Unauthenticated LoadBalancer, WORM Bucket IAM Access, and Unchained `/v1/infra/events` (`F-49`, `F-69`–`F-76`, `F-79`, `F-85`)

**Control:** NIST AC-6 (Least Privilege), CM-7 (Least Functionality), IA-3 (Device Identification and Authentication), SC-7 (Boundary Protection), SI-4 (System Monitoring), AU-9 (Protection of Audit Information)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open — verified across [`src/agentsight-ui/`](../src/agentsight-ui/Dockerfile), `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/agentsight-daemon.yaml`, [`infra/modules/agentsight_ui/`](../infra/modules/agentsight_ui/main.tf), [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf), and [`src/compliance_bridge/main.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/main.py) on `2026-11-05` (`POAM-2026-194` was folded into `POAM-2026-167` Closure Criterion 4; ClickHouse RBAC isolation merged in `#663` `24a10b14` and `ClickHouseSink` retirement merged in `#667` `4c48d107`)
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **Zero active `agentsight_*` producers (`AS-0`, `F-68`):** As recorded in reopened `POAM-021` ([`POAM_US_FED.md`](compliance/us_fed/POAM_US_FED.md)), nothing in `src/` or `deployment/` emits `agentsight_*` eBPF events, and the unmanaged `agentsight` DaemonSet has been refused by Binary Authorization on `cage-staging` since `2026-09-06` (Cloud Audit Logs).
2. **Privileged DaemonSet and host namespace sharing (`F-69`, `F-70`):** `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/agentsight-daemon.yaml` requests `privileged: true`, `hostPID: true`, `CAP_SYS_ADMIN`, `CAP_BPF`, and mounts `/sys/kernel/debug` and `/sys/fs/bpf` while pinned to a placeholder image digest (`sha256:e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855`, the SHA-256 of the empty string).
3. **Unauthenticated external `LoadBalancer` UI (`F-71`–`F-75`, `F-49`):** [`infra/modules/agentsight_ui/main.tf`](../infra/modules/agentsight_ui/main.tf) exposes `agentsight-ui` via an internet-facing `LoadBalancer` Service on plain HTTP port `8080` with `NEXTAUTH_SECRET` unreferenced by the container and `CLICKHOUSE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8123"` in [`staging.tfvars`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/staging.tfvars). (ClickHouse's built-in `default` superuser exposure was remediated separately in `#663` `24a10b14`, scoping `langfuse_app` strictly to `langfuse.*`, and `#667` `4c48d107` retired `4c48d107^:src/compliance_bridge/clickhouse_sink.py`; ClickHouse itself remains in `infra/modules/clickhouse_operator/` as Langfuse v3's OLAP store.)
4. **Unnecessary WORM bucket IAM binding and stdout-only `/v1/infra/events` (`F-85`):** [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/iam.tf) grants `google_service_account.agentsight` (`cage-agentsight-ui`) `roles/storage.objectViewer` on `module.worm_bucket` even though [`src/agentsight-ui/`](../src/agentsight-ui/Dockerfile) never reads from GCS, and `POST /v1/infra/events` in [`src/compliance_bridge/main.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/main.py) logs caller-supplied infrastructure events only to container stdout after `#667` without writing KMS-signed WORM batches via `EvidenceCustodian`.

**Remediation Plan (`§11a` / `R-3`, `AS-1`–`AS-5`, `D-AS-9` / `R-9`):**
1. Retire AgentSight cleanly under the Reference Architecture principle (*Clean Architecture Over Operational Continuity*): delete `src/agentsight-ui/`, `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/agentsight-daemon.yaml`, `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/cilium/README.md` (`cilium-l7-audit-policy.yaml`), and [`infra/modules/agentsight_ui/`](../infra/modules/agentsight_ui/main.tf); remove `module.agentsight_ui` and `google_service_account.agentsight` (including its WORM bucket IAM binding) from `infra/targets/gcp-gke/`; migrate Prometheus evidence-pipeline metrics to the compliance bridge (`D-AS-9`, `R-9`); and either batch `/v1/infra/events` into `EvidenceCustodian` WORM storage or retire unverified event claims in `PR-7c`.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. All AgentSight UI/DaemonSet modules, manifests, and `google_service_account.agentsight` bindings are removed (`§11a` / `R-3`), `/v1/infra/events` WORM custody or OSCAL claim retirement is complete, `terraform test` and Gate G9 pass, and the merge commit SHA is recorded here.

### POAM-2026-195: Unapplied Static Network/Mesh Policies, Shared Data-Store Egress in `network_policy.tf`, and Missing OPA/NeMo `securityContext` (`F-16`–`F-20`, `F-102`)

**Control:** NIST SC-7 (Boundary Protection), AC-3 (Access Enforcement), AC-4 (Information Flow Enforcement), CM-7 (Least Functionality)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open — verified across `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf), [`infra/modules/service_mesh/main.tf`](../infra/modules/service_mesh/main.tf), [`infra/modules/opa_policy/main.tf`](../infra/modules/opa_policy/main.tf), [`infra/modules/gateway/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gateway/main.tf), and [`infra/modules/nemo_guardrails/main.tf`](../infra/modules/nemo_guardrails/main.tf) on `2026-11-05`
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **Static manifests under `deployment/k8s/` are never applied by Terraform (`F-16`, `F-18`, `F-20`):** `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/network-policy.yaml`, `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/network-policy-hardening.yaml`, `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/linkerd-mtls-policy.yaml`, and `de5534ac:deployment/k8s/cilium/egress-lockdown.yaml` are cited as `implemented` evidence in OSCAL `SC-7`/`AC-3`/`AC-4` (`POAM-2026-169` instance 2) but no Terraform resource applies them.
2. **Inline `network_policy.tf` omits ingress isolation and shares data-store egress across all pods (`F-16`, `F-18`):** `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) defines egress-only `NetworkPolicy` resources (except for `reconciler_isolation`), and `default_deny_external_egress` permits **every** pod in `governance-stack` to egress to `10.0.0.0/8` on TCP `5432` (Cloud SQL), `6379` (both governance and app Memorystore Valkey instances), `8080`, `8000`, and `8181`, so the untrusted advisor pod (`governed-advisor`) can connect directly to Cloud SQL (`5432`) and governance Valkey (`6379`) at the network layer.
3. **Linkerd `defaultInboundPolicy = "all-unauthenticated"` and missing OPA/NeMo/bridge `Server` & `AuthorizationPolicy` resources (`F-17`, `F-19`):** [`infra/modules/service_mesh/main.tf`](../infra/modules/service_mesh/main.tf) sets `defaultInboundPolicy = "all-unauthenticated"`, `mesh_policy_enabled` defaults to `false` in [`infra/modules/gateway/variables.tf`](../infra/modules/gateway/variables.tf), and no Terraform resource creates Linkerd `Server` / `AuthorizationPolicy` objects for `opa`, `nemo-guardrails`, or `compliance-bridge`.
4. **Missing pod and container `securityContext` on `opa` and `nemo-guardrails` (`F-102`):** Both `opa` deployments ([`infra/modules/opa_policy/main.tf`](../infra/modules/opa_policy/main.tf) and the gateway's `opa` deployment in [`infra/modules/gateway/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gateway/main.tf)) and `nemo-guardrails` ([`infra/modules/nemo_guardrails/main.tf`](../infra/modules/nemo_guardrails/main.tf)) omit pod and container `security_context` blocks (`run_as_non_root = true`, `read_only_root_filesystem = true`, `allow_privilege_escalation = false`, `capabilities { drop = ["ALL"] }`, `seccomp_profile { type = "RuntimeDefault" }`), and `module.opa` creates a duplicate `opa` Deployment/Service in `governance-stack` alongside `module.gateway`'s `opa` (`§10b` / `R-15`).

**Remediation Plan (`PR-7a`, `PR-7b`, `§10b` / `R-15`):**
1. **`PR-7a` & `§10b` (`R-15`):** Deduplicate `module.opa` vs. `module.gateway`'s `opa` (`§10b`), add hardened pod and container `security_context` blocks to `opa` and `nemo-guardrails` (`F-102`), enable `mesh_policy_enabled = true` by default in enforcing postures (`F-17`), set `defaultInboundPolicy = "all-authenticated"`, and materialize Linkerd `Server` and `AuthorizationPolicy` resources for `gateway`, `compliance-bridge`, `opa`, and `nemo-guardrails` in Terraform (`F-19`).
2. **`PR-7b`:** Replace the shared `10.0.0.0/8` data-store egress rule in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/network_policy.tf) with default-deny ingress + per-workload ingress/egress `NetworkPolicy` rules restricting Cloud SQL (`5432`) egress to `langfuse` and `compliance-bridge` and governance Valkey (`6379`) egress to `gateway`, `compliance-bridge`, and `reconciliation-worker`, and delete the unapplied static `deployment/k8s/network-policy*.yaml` and `linkerd-mtls-policy.yaml` manifests (`F-16`, `F-18`, `F-20`).

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `PR-7a`, `PR-7b`, and `§10b` (`R-15`) merge with `terraform test` and pytest guards verifying per-workload ingress/egress `NetworkPolicy` isolation, Linkerd `Server`/`AuthorizationPolicy` coverage, and hardened `security_context` blocks across all governance workloads.

### POAM-2026-196: Dual-Control Escalation Workload-SVID / Empty-Allowlist Bypass, Unset `initiator_urn`, Optional WebAuthn Binding, and Shared Langfuse Secrets (`F-40`–`F-46`, `F-84`, `R-2`)

**Control:** NIST AC-3 (Access Enforcement), AC-5 (Separation of Duties), IA-2 (Identification and Authentication), AU-9 (Protection of Audit Information)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open — verified in [`src/compliance_bridge/auth.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/auth.py), [`src/compliance_bridge/main.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/main.py), [`src/gateway/governance/governor/verdicts.py`](../src/gateway/governance/governor/verdicts.py), [`src/gateway/governance/defer_queue.py`](../src/gateway/governance/defer_queue.py), and [`infra/modules/gateway/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gateway/main.tf) on `2026-11-05`
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
Four defects weaken dual-control HITL escalation and telemetry-credential separation:
1. **Workload SVID acceptance, empty-allowlist bypass, and `agw_adapter` OIDC delegation in `require_operator_identity()` (`F-40`–`F-44`):** On `POST /v1/defer/{defer_id}/escalate` ([`src/compliance_bridge/main.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/main.py) `line 2288`), `require_operator_identity()` in [`src/compliance_bridge/auth.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/auth.py) (`lines 180–305`) accepts Linkerd workload SVIDs (`l5d-client-id`) as human operator identities (`spiffe://...`), skips the operator allowlist check whenever `CAGE_TRUSTED_OPERATOR_IDENTITIES` is unset (`_trusted_operator_identities()` returns an empty `frozenset()` at `lines 93–96`, and `lines 251–252, 299–300` guard the check behind `if trusted and ...`), and delegated OIDC bearer-token validation (`CAGE_OPERATOR_IDENTITY_ALLOW_OIDC=true`) to `385e502e:src/gateway/governance/ingress/agw_adapter.py` (`385e502e:src/compliance_bridge/auth.py`, replaced in `AR-1` / `POAM-2026-189` by [`src/compliance_bridge/oidc_verifier.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/oidc_verifier.py)).
2. **`initiator_urn` left unset at park time (`F-41`, `AC-5`):** `_park_defer_context()` in [`src/gateway/governance/governor/verdicts.py`](../src/gateway/governance/governor/verdicts.py) (`lines 304–310`) never populates `initiator_urn` on the parked `DeferToken`, neutralizing self-approval rejection (`operator_urn == token.initiator_urn`) inside `DeferQueue.approve()`.
3. **Optional WebAuthn challenge binding (`F-45`, `POAM-2026-169` Instance 13):** `ApprovalRecord` and `DeferQueue.approve()` in [`src/gateway/governance/defer_queue.py`](../src/gateway/governance/defer_queue.py) (`lines 271–335`) leave `client_data_json` and `challenge_binding` optional (`str | None = None`), and `defer_escalate()` in [`src/compliance_bridge/main.py`](../src/compliance_bridge/main.py) (`lines 2292–2296`) accepts and forwards neither field while [`docs/architecture/DEFERRAL_QUEUE.md`](architecture/DEFERRAL_QUEUE.md) previously claimed WebAuthn binding was mandatory.
4. **Shared `advisor-secrets` Langfuse credentials across `gateway` and `governed-advisor` (`F-84`, `R-2`):** [`infra/modules/gateway/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gateway/main.tf) mounts `LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY` and `LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY` from `advisor-secrets`, sharing Langfuse write credentials with the untrusted `governed-advisor` pod so advisor compromise can forge gateway-attributed Langfuse traces/scores.

**Remediation Plan (`PR-5`):**
1. Require a non-empty `CAGE_TRUSTED_OPERATOR_IDENTITIES` allowlist in enforcing postures, authenticate human operators via an in-house OIDC JWT/JWKS verifier on `escalate` (decoupled from `agw_adapter.py` and rejecting workload SVIDs unless explicitly configured for dev/test), populate `initiator_urn` from the verified caller identity in `_park_defer_context()`, require `client_data_json` and `challenge_binding` on `POST /v1/defer/{defer_id}/escalate` in enforcing postures, and provision a dedicated `gateway-secrets` Kubernetes Secret so `gateway` and `governed-advisor` never share Langfuse credentials (`F-84`, `R-2`).

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `PR-5` merges with unit and integration tests covering fail-closed empty `CAGE_TRUSTED_OPERATOR_IDENTITIES`, in-house OIDC operator verification, `initiator_urn` self-approval rejection, mandatory WebAuthn binding in enforcing postures, and separate `gateway-secrets` vs. `advisor-secrets` credentials, and the merge commit SHA is recorded here.

### POAM-2026-197: Ephemeral Workload-Project WORM/CMEK Coupling Across Rebuilds, Unarchived Public-Key Manifests, and Disabled KMS `DATA_READ` Audit Logs (`F-94`, `F-96`, `F-97`, `R-10`)

**Control:** NIST AU-9 (Protection of Audit Information), AU-11 (Audit Record Retention), AU-12 (Audit Record Generation), SC-12 (Cryptographic Key Establishment and Management), SC-28 (Protection of Information at Rest)
**Risk Level:** Moderate
**Status:** Open — verified in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf), [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/kms_signing.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/kms_signing.tf), [`infra/modules/worm_bucket/main.tf`](../infra/modules/worm_bucket/main.tf), and [`src/gateway/governance/evidence/stream.py`](../src/gateway/governance/evidence/stream.py) on `2026-11-05`
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-05
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **WORM bucket and CMEK key live inside the ephemeral workload project (`F-94`, `F-97`):** While `module.worm_bucket` ([`infra/modules/worm_bucket/main.tf`](../infra/modules/worm_bucket/main.tf)) locks retention (`is_locked = local.enforcing_posture`: 1 day in `staging`, 2555 days in `prod`) and [`infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/variables.tf) defaults `kms_signing_protection_level = "HSM"`, both the WORM bucket (`gs://<PROJECT_ID>-evidence-worm-*`) and the symmetric CMEK key (`cage-cmek-<env>`) reside inside the ephemeral workload project `var.project_id`. Tearing down a staging project deletes WORM objects once the 24-hour staging lock expires, and destroying or rotating away the workload project's CMEK key cryptographically shreds archived WORM objects (`R-10`, `AS-6`).
2. **Unarchived `kid` → PEM public-key manifests and unlinked `GENESIS` records across cluster rebuilds (`F-97`):** Signing public-key manifests (`kid` → PEM) are not archived in WORM storage alongside attested evidence batches, and `EvidenceStreamSink` ([`src/gateway/governance/evidence/stream.py`](../src/gateway/governance/evidence/stream.py)) initializes a fresh `GENESIS` record after a cluster/Valkey rebuild without binding `previous_chain_head_hash` or `previous_batch_attestation_digest` from the prior WORM chain head.
3. **Missing Cloud KMS `DATA_READ` audit logging (`F-96`, `POAM-2026-169` Instance 11):** Cloud KMS `AsymmetricSign` is a GCP `DATA_READ` Data Access audit event (disabled by default in GCP), and `infra/targets/gcp-gke/` defines no `google_project_iam_audit_config` for `cloudkms.googleapis.com` and no `google_logging_project_sink` exporting KMS signing logs to retention-locked storage beyond `_Default`'s 30-day retention.

**Remediation Plan (`PR-10` / `R-10`, `AS-6`):**
1. Support an external foundational audit-custody project (`audit_project_id`, `AS-6`) decoupling WORM storage and CMEK/signing key custody from ephemeral workload-cluster teardown, archive `kid` → PEM public-key manifests into WORM storage, bind post-rebuild `GENESIS` records to the prior WORM chain head when continuing an existing archive, enable `google_project_iam_audit_config` (`DATA_READ`, `ADMIN_READ`, `DATA_WRITE`) for `cloudkms.googleapis.com` with a retention-locked log sink (`F-96`), and restate OSCAL `AU-9`/`AU-11`/`AU-12`/`SC-12`/`SC-28` narratives (`R-10`).

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `PR-10` (`R-10`, `AS-6`) merges with `google_project_iam_audit_config` for `cloudkms.googleapis.com`, cross-rebuild key-manifest/chain-head continuity, updated OSCAL statements passing [`scripts/check_oscal_claim_census.py`](../scripts/check_oscal_claim_census.py), and clean-walk verification (`W`).

### POAM-2026-198: Hardcoded Finance OPA Policy in `infra/targets/gcp-gke`, Unrefused Stub Domains in Enforcing Postures, and Unscanned Domain-Literal Scripts (`§10b`)

**Control:** NIST CM-6 (Configuration Settings), AC-3 (Access Enforcement), SA-11 (Developer Testing and Evaluation)
**Risk Level:** High
**Status:** Open — verified in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf), [`scripts/check_policy_drift.py`](../scripts/check_policy_drift.py), `385e502e:scripts/canary_opa_policy.sh`, `385e502e^:scripts/run_matrix_tests.sh`, and [`scripts/check_domain_literals.py`](../scripts/check_domain_literals.py) on `2026-11-07`
**Date Opened:** 2026-11-07
**Target Closure:** 2026-11-30

**Description:**
1. **`module "opa"` hardcoded `cage_finance` Rego regardless of `var.cage_domain`:** `385e502e:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (`lines 951–953`) mounted `file("${path.module}/../../../src/cage_finance/opa/trade_governance.rego")` into `module "opa"` unconditionally even when `var.cage_domain` was set to `"healthcare"` or `"physical_ai"` (`variables.tf` validates `contains(["finance", "healthcare", "physical_ai"], var.cage_domain)`), and `module "opa"` duplicates the `opa` sidecar/deployment inside [`infra/modules/gateway/main.tf`](../infra/modules/gateway/main.tf) (`POAM-2026-195`).
2. **Stub domains were not mechanically refused in enforcing postures:** `physical_ai` ([`src/cage_physical_ai/`](../src/cage_physical_ai/__init__.py)) is a non-production reference stub without an `opa/` policy bundle (`POAM-2026-077`), whereas `finance` ([`src/cage_finance/`](../src/cage_finance/__init__.py)) and `healthcare` ([`src/cage_healthcare/`](../src/cage_healthcare/__init__.py)) ship `maturity = "production"` plugins with OPA policy bundles (`trade.governance`, `dosing.governance`), yet neither `infra/targets/gcp-gke/` nor `assemble_governor()` (`is_enforcing()`) mechanically refused `maturity == "stub"` or empty domain Rego bundles.
3. **Operational scripts hardcoded finance/legacy policy paths outside Gate G6:** `385e502e:scripts/check_policy_drift.py` (`lines 65–67` `_DEFAULT_INPUT` / `_DEFAULT_OPA_OUT`), `385e502e:scripts/canary_opa_policy.sh` (`lines 22–24` `POLICY_FILE` / `OPA_BUNDLE_ROOT`), and `385e502e:scripts/run_matrix_tests.sh` (`line 267` `src/cage_finance/opa/`) hardcoded finance or unparameterized legacy policy paths while `385e502e:scripts/check_domain_literals.py` (`Gate G6`) did not scan `scripts/`.

**Remediation Plan (`Wave 2.7` / `§10b` & `R-15`):**
1. Parameterize `opa_policies` in `infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf` (49c0d09a:infra/targets/gcp-gke/main.tf) via `fileset("${path.module}/../../../src/cage_${var.cage_domain}/opa", "*.rego")` excluding `*_test.rego` (verified in [`domain_opa_policies.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/domain_opa_policies.tftest.hcl) for `finance`, `healthcare`, and `physical_ai` refusal), test `OPAClient.verify_domain_policy()` fail-closed rejection on wrong-domain policy bundles, and deduplicate `module "opa"` vs. `module.gateway` (`R-15`).
2. Add `CagePlugin.maturity: Literal["production", "stub"]` so `assemble_governor()` refuses `"stub"` plugins whenever `is_enforcing(posture)` is true, and enforce non-empty domain `.rego` bundle preconditions on `terraform_data.domain_opa_policies_guard` (`module.opa` `depends_on`, `POAM-2026-180`) and `kubernetes_config_map.opa_policies` in [`infra/modules/opa_policy/main.tf`](../infra/modules/opa_policy/main.tf).
3. Parameterize [`scripts/check_policy_drift.py`](../scripts/check_policy_drift.py) (`--domain`), retire `385e502e:scripts/canary_opa_policy.sh` and `385e502e^:scripts/run_matrix_tests.sh` (live posture checks folded into [`scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh`](../scripts/staging_lifecycle.sh) Phases 3–5), and extend [`scripts/check_domain_literals.py`](../scripts/check_domain_literals.py) (`Gate G6`) to scan non-test operational scripts.

**Closure Criteria:**
1. `Wave 2.7` (`§10b`) and `R-15` merge with [`domain_opa_policies.tftest.hcl`](../infra/targets/gcp-gke/tests/domain_opa_policies.tftest.hcl) (positive and negative assertions), `CagePlugin.maturity` runtime refusal tests, `OPAClient.verify_domain_policy()` mismatch refusal tests, and expanded Gate G6 coverage, and the merge commit SHA is recorded here.
